# I-2 integration custody

Registration: BRIEF_UNDERSTAND sections 168–195, especially 190–195 and clarification 1 at 4cde021d. Worktree: wt-i2, branch codex/i2-tracks. This document records operations; it does not amend scientific rules.

I-2 owns C/E73, G/E74, and conditional Ω/E77. Track Q, E76 and E78 remain with the existing integrator. W-census and W-glm are separate existing chats; do not duplicate their implementation. W-lens owns both prospective forecasts and E75 pooling. Worker chat identifiers and current states are in STATUS.json.

## Admission and custody

1. Before capture: C's sampler, frozen title list, source revisions and BOW exclusions must be committed. G's ASSET_CARD must identify the announced assets and pass anonymous weights/licence access and at most 8 GPUs per replica; no replacement model or lens. G's registered smoke test is at most 20 GPU-minutes and stops on setup mismatch.
2. Each pod receives only its own source/input shard, at most 20 MB. Verify exact archive hashes, actual members, privacy exclusion and isolated CPU preflight before upload. No labels, KEY or PRIVATE material may go to pods. Staging has a separate 900-second ceiling; its cost still counts.
3. At each launch, reconcile Track Q and I-2 final billing and outstanding maximum liabilities, then publish the combined projected USD total. Include GPU count, current rate, staging, compute, waits and closeout in reservations. Ceiling is USD300 per Zurich day. An approximate starting spend is not a durable budget reservation. Refuse an over-ceiling launch and ask Guiv to raise it. Unknown concurrent reservations hold admission.
4. Worker self-admission requires measured forecast multiplied by 1.25 within the registered cap; otherwise NO_FIT. C remains at most 6 aggregate GPU-hours. C and G use different pods. A launch budget does not waive the runtime gate.
5. Capture, verify all seal members, preserve input order/hash, then deliver capture-only material and CPU probes to W-lens. C needs all-position L44 and read-token L42; G uses the exact lens read layer. W-lens commits the matching model's prospective J-forecast before I-2 issues GO and before readers run. Verify forecast bytes at the actual commit, capture/input bindings, and timestamps. Never open reader payloads before that commit.
6. Reader outputs seal and wait. No judging for either bank until Guiv separately authorises its E56-route budget (registered upper bounds USD5 each). Neither this assignment nor a forecast is judging approval.
7. At closeout, verify pull integrity and record private-whitener deletion before release. Commit no keys or pod host details. Every run, including CPU preparation and refusals, gets a track C/G/Ω entry in both shared log.md and compute.jsonl. Secret-scan every commit, git pull --rebase, no force push.

## Coordination

The live Track Q operational mailbox is results/g_agreement/r/HANDOFF.md in the phase-r-integration-8abda6 checkout under the Neel_Exploration repository. Its header explicitly instructs streams to append one dated handoff note, never rewrite others. Its results/g_agreement/compute.jsonl is ahead of the stale r/blindspot ledger. Use both, preserving provenance and avoiding duplicate charges. Before launch, obtain a fresh reservation reconciliation from Q; a historical read is insufficient against concurrent launches. Append I-2 reservations to that mailbox. Keep pod connection data private and out of these files.

Reusable mechanics are on r/blindspot-mech in results/g_agreement/understand/replan402 (worker.py, build.py, preflight.py), but are hardcoded to prior banks, one GPU and Qwen width. Do not execute them unchanged for C/G. Older joint9 includes obsolete USD150 rules. W-lens's existing forecast_once.py needs explicit C/G extensions and model bindings. I-2's CPU validator is an additional review check, never an automatic GO or authority to change a rule.

## Timing

- G ASSET_CARD due 2026-10-27 15:55:30 Europe/Zurich (30 minutes after task creation).
- At 2026-10-28 12:00 Europe/Zurich: open Ω only if C already has sealed captures and G has a capture running at that instant. Otherwise append exactly `E77 not run (gate)` and a zero-usage Ω event. Do not decide early or substitute later progress.
- At 2026-10-28 18:00 Europe/Zurich: no new launches. Already-running jobs finish under their existing caps and custody.
- Two-hour reports to Guiv include per-track state, spend and blockers. The heartbeat and exact gate/freeze wakeups are registered in STATUS.json.
